Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P2-05: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A two-column evidence log with one specific good action by a fictional learner/family member/school worker and the observable effect on another person; include the relevant religious principle from a verified source.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one good action in the evidence log and the person who performed it. This assesses: “ชื่นชมการทำความดีของตนเอง บุคคลในครอบครัวและในโรงเรียนตามหลักศาสนา”. **Answer:** The answer names an action and actor exactly as recorded.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how the action reflects the named religious principle and describe its effect. This assesses: “ชื่นชมการทำความดีของตนเอง บุคคลในครอบครัวและในโรงเรียนตามหลักศาสนา”. **Answer:** The explanation connects the action to a source-supported principle and a plausible effect shown in the log.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “ชื่นชมการทำความดีของตนเอง บุคคลในครอบครัวและในโรงเรียนตามหลักศาสนา”. **Answer:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.
### 37. SO1.1-P2-06

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-06` — เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P2-06: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A verified instruction card for a short attention or loving-kindness practice taught in the selected tradition, listing steps and the stated purpose. Offer observation/explanation instead of participation.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the practice and place its steps in the order given. This assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** The response names and sequences the steps accurately from the card.
2. **Explain / compare:** Which step helps the learner return attention or direct kind thoughts, according to the source? Explain its purpose. This assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** The learner identifies the relevant step and explains the purpose using the source; no assessment of inner state or sincerity.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Select a practice taught in the verified tradition card and, voluntarily, demonstrate or sequence its steps; alternatively, annotate the steps and explain their purpose as an observer. This task assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Steps and purpose are source-accurate; demonstration is voluntary and observation/explanation is accepted without grading belief or spiritual attainment.
### 38. SO1.1-P2-07

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-07` — บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P2-07: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A comparison chart of at least two religions, with each tradition's name, founder/central figure where applicable, and one named scripture/canonical text plus its role, verified against reliable sources.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the chart to name the traditions and match each one to its founder/central figure and text. This assesses: “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** All matches must agree with the chart and verified sources; acknowledge traditions where founder terminology is not applicable.
2. **Explain / compare:** Choose one text from the chart and explain its importance to adherents using the information provided. This assesses: “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** The explanation describes the text's role accurately and respectfully; do not rank traditions or assume every adherent relates to a text identically.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.
### 39. SO1.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A role-play card showing a respectful interaction with a religious teacher/monastic or other recognised religious adherent, with source/school guidance on appropriate greeting, listening and boundaries.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify two appropriate behaviours shown in the role-play card. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** The answer names two behaviours that match the card and local school guidance.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why one behaviour is respectful and identify one behaviour to avoid. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** The explanation links behaviour to respect; the avoided behaviour must be specific and non-discriminatory.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Role-play a respectful interaction with a religious adherent using the context-specific guidance card; demonstrate the appropriate greeting or conduct only where the source says it is appropriate. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Observed interaction follows the supplied tradition-specific guidance and respects the person's boundaries; do not assume one greeting or practice fits all.
### 40. SO1.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-02` — ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A verified observance/ceremony card with its name, purpose, date/context and ordered age-appropriate actions, drawn from the tradition being taught.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the observance and identify two steps from the verified sequence. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** The response identifies the correct event and two accurate steps.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the purpose of one step and state one respectful classroom behaviour for learners who observe or do not observe it. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Purpose matches the source; behaviour protects inclusion and does not compel participation.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** In a scenario based on the verified event card, demonstrate or sequence appropriate conduct for a participant or observer and explain why it fits that event. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Conduct and explanation match the named event and source; observing is acceptable and worship participation is never compulsory.
### 41. SO2.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** A class has 4 shared storybooks, 6 learners who want to read, and one classroom rule: return a book before another learner borrows it. The teacher adds one short case detail matched to the indicator (family role, classroom duty, community decision, cultural practice, rights or public holiday).

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the case card. Identify the person, group, right, duty, rule, custom or decision-making step that is directly relevant to “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Give one detail from the case as evidence. **Answer:** Identify the relevant person/group/concept or step and cite the matching detail in the case. The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Complete a short classroom routine simulation by following the relevant agreement/rule, then point to the rule that guided the action. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** The learner performs the action consistently with the stated rule and distinguishes it from a personal preference.
### 42. SO2.1-P2-02

The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Role-play a respectful response to a new, realistic school/community interaction; the observer records the specific behaviour shown rather than judging accent, status or personality. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนตามมารยาทไทย”. **Answer:** The behaviour is appropriate to the stated context and respects personal boundaries; accept culturally and situationally appropriate variation.
### 43. SO2.1-P2-03

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-03` — แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** A class has 4 shared storybooks, 6 learners who want to read, and one classroom rule: return a book before another learner borrows it. The teacher adds one short case detail matched to the indicator (family role, classroom duty, community decision, cultural practice, rights or public holiday).

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the case card. Identify the person, group, right, duty, rule, custom or decision-making step that is directly relevant to “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ”. Give one detail from the case as evidence. **Answer:** Identify the relevant person/group/concept or step and cite the matching detail in the case. The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** In a paired discussion, demonstrate listening to a different view, restating it fairly and responding without ridicule or exclusion. This task assesses: “แสดงพฤติกรรมในการยอมรับความคิด ความเชื่อ และการปฏิบัติของบุคคลอื่นที่แตกต่างกันโดยปราศจากอคติ”. **Answer:** Teacher observes accurate listening and a non-discriminatory response; learners are not required to agree with the other view.

### 47. SO3.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-01` — ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — family money record:** This week a learner receives 50 baht. Recorded spending: snack 10 baht, school pencil 5 baht, bus fare 10 baht. The learner plans to save the money left.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”, and give a reason. **Answer:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.

- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — family money record:** This week a learner receives 50 baht. Recorded spending: snack 10 baht, school pencil 5 baht, bus fare 10 baht. The learner plans to save the money left.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Complete a dated practice ledger from supplied, age-appropriate transactions, recording each income/expense and calculating the closing balance; use fictional amounts if preferred. This task assesses: “บันทึกรายรับ-รายจ่ายของตนเอง”. **Answer:** Every transaction is entered in the correct column and the balance is arithmetically correct; no learner must disclose personal finances.

- **Official indicator:** สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-04` — สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Assessment stimulus — family money record:** This week a learner receives 50 baht. Recorded spending: snack 10 baht, school pencil 5 baht, bus fare 10 baht. The learner plans to save the money left.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “สรุปผลดีของการใช้จ่ายที่เหมาะสมกับรายได้และการออม”, and give a reason. **Answer:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.

### 53. SO4.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P2-01` — ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Prepare three labelled evidence cards about the family, school or community topic named in the lesson: (A) an older photograph/object or first-person account, (B) a dated record or reliable written source, and (C) a present-day observation. Write the source and date on each card; do not invent dates or historical claims.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Examine evidence cards A–C. Identify the event, person, time, change, symbol or historical detail needed for “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. State which card supports your answer. **Answer:** Name a detail that is explicitly supported by the cited card and identify the source correctly. If the indicator asks for sequence or calendar time, the order/date must agree with the evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two of the evidence cards. Explain one similarity, difference, sequence or cause-and-effect relationship that helps answer “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. **Answer:** The response accurately compares the chosen sources and states one supported relationship. Credit a correct time sequence or a plausible cause/effect only when supported by the evidence; distinguish evidence from opinion.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต”. Include the source label for each factual detail. **Answer:** The product includes the required number/order of steps or details, is consistent with the evidence cards, and identifies sources. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.

2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two of the evidence cards. Explain one similarity, difference, sequence or cause-and-effect relationship that helps answer “สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** The response accurately compares the chosen sources and states one supported relationship. Credit a correct time sequence or a plausible cause/effect only when supported by the evidence; distinguish evidence from opinion.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “สืบค้นถึงการเปลี่ยนแปลงในวิถีชีวิตประจำวันของคนในชุมชนของตนจากอดีตถึงปัจจุบัน”. Include the source label for each factual detail. **Answer:** The product includes the required number/order of steps or details, is consistent with the evidence cards, and identifies sources. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.
### 56. SO4.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P2-02` — อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Prepare three labelled evidence cards about the family, school or community topic named in the lesson: (A) an older photograph/object or first-person account, (B) a dated record or reliable written source, and (C) a present-day observation. Write the source and date on each card; do not invent dates or historical claims.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Examine evidence cards A–C. Identify the event, person, time, change, symbol or historical detail needed for “อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน”. If the indicator asks for sequence or calendar time, the order/date must agree with the evidence.

Credit a correct time sequence or a plausible cause/effect only when supported by the evidence; distinguish evidence from opinion.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a two- or three-step timeline, labelled explanation or short oral-history summary that answers “อธิบายผลกระทบของการเปลี่ยนแปลงที่มีต่อวิถีชีวิตของคนในชุมชน”. Do not award factual credit for unsupported invented history.

### 59. SO5.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P2-01` — ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “สังเกตและแสดงความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างโลก ดวงอาทิตย์และดวงจันทร์ที่ทำให้เกิดปรากฏการณ์”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “สังเกตและแสดงความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างโลก ดวงอาทิตย์และดวงจันทร์ที่ทำให้เกิดปรากฏการณ์”. **Answer:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Use a safe teacher-led model or diagram to show the relative positions/movement of Earth, Sun and Moon for the phenomenon being taught; narrate what is moving relative to what. This task assesses: “สังเกตและแสดงความสัมพันธ์ระหว่างโลก ดวงอาทิตย์และดวงจันทร์ที่ทำให้เกิดปรากฏการณ์”. **Answer:** Model demonstrates the scientifically correct relationship for the named phenomenon; never ask learners to look directly at the Sun.
### 62. SO5.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO5.2
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Objective:** `SO5.2-P2-01` — อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. **Answer:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “อธิบายความสำคัญของสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).

- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.

Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Join a supervised school environmental action, complete an assigned role and record one before/after indicator (for example, litter count in a defined area). This task assesses: “มีส่วนร่วมในการจัดการสิ่งแวดล้อมในโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** Learner completes a safe role and records a comparable measure; the class uses the same area and method before and after.
### 66. SO1.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P3-01` — อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A source set with one documented Thai cultural practice, a description of the relevant religious idea/tradition and a reliable source/date. Avoid claiming a single religion explains all Thai culture.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the cultural practice and the religious tradition or teaching that the source explicitly connects to it. This assesses: “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer:** Both the practice and connection must be stated in the source; accept a qualified answer that notes multiple influences.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one way the documented tradition has influenced the practice, citing the source evidence. This assesses: “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer:** The answer gives a supported influence and cites a concrete detail; do not generalise beyond the evidence.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “อธิบายความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ในฐานะที่เป็นรากฐานสำคัญของวัฒนธรรมไทย”. **Answer:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.
### 67. SO1.1-P3-02

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P3-02` — สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P3-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A verified timeline of the Buddha's period of ascetic practice through enlightenment/teaching and parinibbana, or equivalent key events for the selected tradition's founder; label source and chronology.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Arrange the events from ascetic practice to parinibbana (or the source's equivalent life stages) in correct order. This assesses: “สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Chronology and event names match the verified timeline.
2. **Explain / compare:** Select one event and explain its place in the wider life story using one earlier and one later event. This assesses: “สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** The explanation correctly situates the event between source-supported events; do not add unsupported dates.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Case: A class is preparing a display about the source card. A learner suggests an action that shows the idea respectfully in everyday school life. Describe one suitable action and explain how it connects to “สรุปพุทธประวัติตั้งแต่การบำเพ็ญเพียร จนถึงปรินิพพาน หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** One feasible action is named and explicitly linked to an accurate idea from the source card. Accept different actions when the link is valid. Never grade a learner's faith, identity, worship or religious participation.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A verified reference card defining the Three Jewels and Ovada 3, or the selected tradition's corresponding approved teaching, with one accurate example for each principle.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the relevant teaching and identify the principle represented by each example on the card. This assesses: “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** All examples are matched to the correct source-defined principle.
2. **Explain / compare:** For a fictional playground conflict, demonstrate a response based on one principle and explain the connection. This assesses: “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** The response is safe and observable and its explanation correctly connects to the selected principle; never assess personal belief.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Use a verified principle card to demonstrate a fair response in a role-played everyday situation, then identify the principle and explain the link. This task assesses: “แสดงความเคารพพระรัตนตรัยและปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** The observable action and explanation match the source-verified principle; assess conduct in the scenario, not private belief or worship.

4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card: A source card describing a specific attention, loving-kindness or mental-development practice in the selected tradition, with steps, purpose and an optional non-participatory observation route.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the practice, its steps and the purpose stated in the source. This assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตามีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนา หรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** All details match the verified source; accept a labelled diagram or oral explanation.

3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Choose a practice described in the verified tradition card and voluntarily demonstrate or sequence its steps; alternatively, as an observer, annotate the steps and explain the purpose. This task assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตามีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิ ในพระพุทธศาสนา หรือการพัฒนาจิตตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Steps and purpose match the selected source; demonstration is voluntary and no learner is graded on belief, prayer sincerity or spiritual attainment.
